Adam details:
```
def _adam_optimize(a_init, budget, rng, T0_frac=0.25, T1_frac=0.01, lr=0.5, coord_interval...):
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a_init, 1e-12))
    z -= z.mean()
    m = zeros; v=zeros
    best_a=a_init; best_score=score
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    T = T0_frac * maxC
    while time < deadline:
       a = softmax(z)
       c = autocorr(a)
       mc = max(c)
       if update best
       # adjust T: if no_improve, increase T; else decrease
       p = softmax_c(c, T)
       s = 2 * weighted_gradient? Actually weighted_gradient includes 2 in previous. We'll make returns 2*...
       h = s - np.dot(a,s)
       scale = max(1e-12, np.std(h))
       m = b1*m + (1-b1)*(h/scale); v = b2*v + (1-b2)*(h/scale)^2
       z = z - lr * mhat / sqrt(vhat+eps)
       z -= z.max(); z = clip(z, -60, 45)
       # coordinate polish occasionally
```
But if h is raw gradient wrt z? For softmax, gradient wrt z is a_i (s_i - mean_a s). Pseudo:
```
def _adam_optimize(a0, budget, rng, tag=""):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    loga -= loga.mean()
    m=zero; v=zero; it=0
    c=autocorr(a); mc=c.max(); best=...
    temp = 0.35
    lr = 0.7
    no_improve=0
    last_polish=time
    while time < deadline:
       c=autocorr(a from loga) # we can update a after each iter
       mc = c.max()
       if score< best: best...; no_improve=0
       # choose p
       if it % 10 ==0 or no_improve>... use topK else softmax
       s = _weighted_gradient(p,a)
       mean = np.dot(a,s)
       h = s - mean
       scale = max(np.max(np.abs(h)), 1e-12)
       h/=scale
       m = b1*m + (1-b1)*h; v=...
       step = lr * m/(sqrt(v)+1e-6)
       # To avoid too large, max step per coordinate maybe 1.0
       np.clip(step, -1.5,1.5, out=step)
       loga = loga - step
       loga -= loga.max()
       np.clip(loga, -60, 60, out=loga)
       a = np.exp(loga); a/=a.sum()
       # occasional coordinate polish if no_improve>...
       if no_improve>30 or time-last_polish > interval:
           ap,sc,cp = _coordinate_polish(a, c, polish_budget, rng)
           if sc < current: adopt; no_improve=0
       # temp adaptive
       if no_improve==0: temp.max(0.01,temp*0.98); lr=max(min_lr,lr*0.995)
       else: no_improve +=1; if no_improve%10==0: temp=min(1.0,temp*1.2); lr=min(2.0,lr*1.05)
    return best
```
But note `c` from before update used for gradient; after update need recompute next loop. At start we have current a. Okay.

Top-K p implementation:
```
def _active_weights(c, mode, K, temp):
    L=c.size; mc=c.max()
    if mode=='softmax': z=(c-mc)/mc/temp; z=clip; p=exp; normalize
    elif mode=='topK': if K>=L: p=c; else: thr=np.partition(c, L-K)[L-K]; p=c.copy(); p[p<thr]=0; if p.sum()==0 p fill; normalize
```
Use topK when no_improve >5 and temp small. Alternatively use violations relative to target: `target = best_score/(2n)`; p = c - target? But target may below current; use max.

```
def _adam_optimize(a0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n<2: return a, inf
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); loga-=loga.mean()
    c=_autocorr(a); mc=max(c); score=2*n*mc
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=1
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999
    lr=0.8; min_lr=0.02; max_lr=2.5
    temp=0.40; min_temp=0.008; max_temp=1.2
    no_improve=0; it=0
    use_top=False; K=0
    polish_interval=5.0; polish_budget=3.0
    last_polish=time.monotonic()
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    while time<deadline:
       if time-last_polish > polish_interval and budget_s - elapsed > 30:
           # run polish if optimal maybe
           ap,sc,cp = _coordinate_polish(a,c, polish_budget, rng)
           last_polish=time.monotonic()
           if sc < 2*n*mc - 1e-14:
              a=_normalize(ap); c=cp; mc=max(c); score=2*n*mc; no_improve=0
              if score<best_score: best...
           # else no_improve++?
       if time>deadline: break
       # prepare p
       if K==0: use softmax
       # maybe use top when no_improve>10
       if no_improve > 10 and n>100:
          K = ... but can reset after improve
       p=_active_weights(c, temp, K, rng)
       s=_weighted_gradient(p,a)
       # mean
       mean = float(np.dot(a,s))
       h=s-mean
       # optional add small regularization to penalize entropy? no.
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
       if not finite or scale<1e-15: scale=1e-15
       h/=scale
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*h
       v=b2*v+(1-b1)*h*h
       # bias correction
       bc1=1-b1**t; bc2=1-b2**t
       step=lr*(m/bc1)/np.sqrt(v/bc2+1e-8)
       np.clip(step, -1.2, 1.2, out=step)
       loga = loga - step
       loga -= np.max(loga)
       np.clip(loga, -55.0, 55.0, out=loga)
       a=np.exp(loga); ssum=a.sum(); a/=ssum
       # compute new c/score
       c=_autocorr(a); mc=float(c.max()); score=2*n*mc
       if score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
          temp=max(min_temp, temp*0.985); lr=min(2.0,lr*1.005?) # maybe keep
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve==1: temp=min(max_temp, temp*1.05)
          if no_improve % 20 ==0: temp=min(max_temp,temp*1.2); lr=min(max_lr,lr*1.1)
          if no_improve > 80:
             # perturb from best
             a= _perturb_log(best_a, rng)
             loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); loga-=max; c=_autocorr(a); mc=max; score...
             m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=1; no_improve=0; temp=0.25; lr=0.6; K=0
       it +=1; t+=1
       if it%2000==0: print score
    # final ensure recompute
    return _normalize(best_a), best_score
```

Let's refine coordinate polish vectorization:
```
def _coordinate_polish(f0, c0, budget_s, rng, topK=128):
  f=_normalize(f0); n=f.size; L=2*n-1
  c = copy if size L else autocorr
  maxc=c.max(); best_f=f.copy(); best_score=2nmaxc
  deadline=...
  D,R=...
  delta=zeros(L); total=zeros(L)
  no_improve=0
  while time<deadline and no_improve<10:
     maxc=float(c.max())
     p=_active_weights(c, temp=0.25, topK=topK)
     g=_weighted_gradient(p,f)
     donor_order=np.argsort(g)[::-1]
     recv_order=np.argsort(g)
     pick top D with f>1e-12; receivers top R not donors
     if none break
     donor_units list; recv_units list
     best_mc=maxc; best_move=None
     for i in donors: ... for j in receivers: if i!=j:
        for frac in fracs:
           dv=fi*frac
           np.add(ui,uj,out=delta); delta*=2dv
           q=dv*dv
           delta[2*j]+=q; delta[2*i]+=q; delta[i+j]-=2q
           np.add(c,delta,out=total); mc=total.max()
           if mc<best_mc: best_mc=mc; best_move=(i,j,dv,delta.copy())
     # random fallback
     ...
     if best_move and best_mc < maxc - 1e-15: apply
     else no_improve++
  recompute final score; return best
```

If 2i == i+j? j=i skip. If 2j==i+j? i=j skip. Good. But if 2i == 2j? skip. Let's implement `_random_shape(n, rng)`:
```
def _random_shape(n, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    u=x-0.5
    z=np.zeros(n)
    # polynomial coefficients
    deg=int(rng.integers(1,6))
    sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.2,2.2))
    for d in range(1, deg+1):
        z += float(rng.normal(0, sigma)) * (u ** d)
    # optional endpoint log
    if rng.random()<0.7:
       z += float(rng.normal(0,1.2))*np.log(x+1e-3) + float(rng.normal(0,1.2))*np.log(1-x+1e-3)
    if rng.random()<0.7:
       m=int(rng.integers(1,4))
       z += float(rng.normal(0,0.8))*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       if rng.random()<0.5: z += float(rng.normal(0,0.6))*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + ...)
    z += float(rng.normal(0,0.5))
    z = np.clip(z, -6, 6)
    a=np.exp(z); a/=a.sum()
    return a
```
Could include monotone ramps: `z = alpha*x^p` p random. The polynomial covers.

Let's test correctness of `_weighted_gradient` with p length L. Implementation:
```
def _weighted_gradient(p, f):
    n=f.size; L=p.size; m=L+n-1; size=_fft_size(m)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1],size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(P*F,size)
    return 2.0*conv[n-1:n+n-1]
```
Check length n. Good.

n=f.size; size=_fft_size(2*n-1); F=np.fft.rfft(f,size); full=np.fft.irfft(F*F,size); return full[:2*n-1]
```

def _clip_z(loga):
    loga = np.asarray(loga)
    loga -= np.max(loga)
    np.clip(loga, -55.0, 55.0, out=loga)
    return loga

def _quick_random_scan(n, rng, time_limit):
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  u=x-0.5
  bases=[]
  bases.append(np.ones(n))
  bases.append(u)
  bases.append(u*u)
  bases.append(u**3)
  bases.append(u**4)
  bases.append(np.log(x+1e-3))
  bases.append(np.log(1-x+1e-3))
  for m in (1,2,3): bases.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x + 0.3)); bases.append(np.cos(...+0.7))
  B=np.array(bases) # shape b,n maybe memory ~12*8192 small
  starts=[]
  deadline=time+time_limit
  count=0
  while time<deadline:
    # random coeffs
    sig=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.8))
    coeff=rng.normal(0, sig, B.shape[0])
    # reduce some coefficients to zero
    coeff[rng.random(B.shape[0])<0.3]=0
    z=B.dot(coeff) # if B shape (m,n)
    z=np.clip(z,-6,6)
    a=np.exp(z); a/=a.sum()
    sc=_fast_score(a)
    if finite:
       starts.append((sc,count,a))
    count +=1
    if count%500==0: # sort / prune
       starts.sort()
       if len(starts)>50: del starts[50:]
    if len(starts)>1000: starts=sorted... maybe
  if starts:
    starts.sort(); return [a for sc,_,a in starts[:10]], best_sc
  return [], inf
```

Now, let's think about an even stronger local method: "active set water-filling with Lagrange multiplier".

Given we want minimize y s.t. P_k <= y. At optimum, for active K, KKT: a_i ∝ 1/(∑_{k∈K} ν_k a_{k-i}). We can perform fixed-point:
```
q_i = ∑_{k active} w_k a_{k-i}
a_new_i ∝ a_i / (q_i + eps)? or 1/(q_i+eps).
```
If we update `loga -= lr * q_i`, that's similar to gradient. But KKT suggests `loga_i + log q_i = const` => `loga_i = C - log q_i`. We can iterate `loga_i <- -log(q_i)` (plus center) with damping. This might converge to exact local optimum faster than gradient. Let's consider: q_i depends on a. Define map a_i <- 1/q_i normalized. Does it reduce max? Maybe. Could implement as an additional optimizer:
```
loga = 0.5*( -log(q+eps) - loga )? 
```
But q_i can be zero; use previous loga. We can try a "dual step": at each iteration, compute p over active peaks; q = weighted_gradient(p,a)/2. Then propose `loga_new = -log(q+eps)`; center. Then line search between current loga and loga_new for several alphas, choosing best score. This has KKT flavor. It may be powerful.

But if q is small for many indices, -log large -> assign mass to low influence (edges), good. If q zero for all but? Use q+eps. Could be unstable. We can incorporate in `_adam_optimize`: occasionally take a line search to `loga_target = -log(q+1e-12)` with step 0.1-0.5. But extra FFTs. Maybe not.

Alternatively use exponentiated gradient with learning rate 1 (mirror descent): `a_i *= 1/q_i`. Could work. Let's not risk instability; Adam stable.

Could use `s = q` and update `loga -= lr*(s - median(s))`. Similar.

Maybe use a fixed "peeling" algorithm:
- Choose top K peaks. Solve for `loga_i = -log(∑_{k∈K} w_k a_{k-i})` (KKT) and normalize with damping. Then project? Since objective may go up; line search. This might jump to better basins.
We can implement a simplified deterministic step in Adam:
```
q = _weighted_gradient(p,a)*0.5
target = -np.log(q + 1e-10)
target -= target.max()
# line search alpha in [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5]
for alpha: cand = normalize(exp(alpha*target + (1-alpha)*loga? or loga + alpha*(target-loga)))
evaluate; pick best
```
This is a convex combination in log-space toward KKT fixed point. It may be more directed than gradient. But extra evaluations (4 FFTs per iter) too heavy. Maybe every 20 iterations. Pseudo code detailed:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027

def _normalize(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if v.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    v[~np.isfinite(v)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(v,0.0,out=v)
    s=float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(v.size)/float(v.size)
    v /= s
    return v

def _fft_size(m):
    if m <= 2: return 2
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def _autocorr(f):
    n=f.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    m=2*n-1
    size=_fft_size(m)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,size)
    full=np.fft.irfft(F*F,size)
    full=np.maximum(full[:m],0.0)
    return full

def _score_norm(a, c=None):
    if c is None: c=_autocorr(a)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()))

def _fast_score(a):
    a=_normalize(a)
    return _score_norm(a)

def _weighted_gradient(p, f):
    n=f.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    L=p.size
    m=L+n-1
    size=_fft_size(m)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1],size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(P*F,size)
    return 2.0*conv[n-1:n+n-1]

def _active_weights(c, temp, topK=0):
    L=c.size
    if L==0: return np.empty(0)
    p=np.zeros(L)
    if topK is None or topK <=0 or topK >= L:
        mc=float(c.max())
        if mc <= 0:
            p[np.argmin(c)] = 1.0? actually any
            return p
        denom = mc*max(1e-12,float(temp))
        z=(c-mc)/denom
        np.clip(z,-80.0,0.0,out=z)
        w=np.exp(z)
        s=float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0:
            p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
        else: p=w/s
    else:
        K=int(min(L,max(1,topK)))
        if K>=L:
            s=float(c.sum())
            if s>0: p=c/s
            else: p.fill(1.0/L)
        else:
            idx=L-K
            thr=float(np.partition(c, idx)[idx])
            p = c.copy()
            p[p < thr] = 0.0
            s=float(p.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300:
                p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
            else: p/=s
    return p
```
If topK small and max repeats, p includes many >K; okay.

`_interp_to_length`:
```
def _interp_to_length(f,n):
    f=_normalize(f); m=f.size
    if n<=0: return np.empty(0)
    if n==1: return np.array([1.0])
    if m==n: return f
    if m==1: return np.ones(n)/n
    x=np.linspace(0,m-1,m); y=np.linspace(0,n-1,n)
    return _normalize(np.interp(y,x,f))
```
For y start 0 end n-1. Good.

`_smooth` maybe not needed.

`_random_shape` top-level:
```
def _random_shape(n, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    u=x-0.5
    z=np.zeros(n)
    deg=int(rng.integers(1,6))
    sig=float(rng.uniform(0.2,2.0))
    for d in range(1,deg+1):
       z += float(rng.normal(0, sig)) * (u ** d)
    if rng.random()<0.75:
       z += float(rng.normal(0,1.2))*np.log(x+1e-3)
       z += float(rng.normal(0,1.2))*np.log(1.0-x+1e-3)
    if rng.random()<0.75:
       m=int(rng.integers(1,4))
       z += float(rng.normal(0,0.8))*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x + float(rng.uniform(0,6.283)))
       if rng.random()<0.5:
          z += float(rng.normal(0,0.6))*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + float(rng.uniform(0,6.283)))
    z += float(rng.normal(0,0.3))
    z=np.clip(z, -6.0, 6.0)
    a=np.exp(z)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: a=np.ones(n)/n
    else: a/=s
    return a
```

`_quick_random_scan(n,rng,time_limit)`:
Precompute bases. But `_random_shape` already computes each; for scan use basis for speed.
```
def _quick_random_scan(n, rng, time_limit):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    u=x-0.5
    bases=[np.ones(n), u, u*u, u**3, u**4, np.log(x+1e-3), np.log(1-x+1e-3)]
    for m in (1,2,3):
       bases.append(np.sin(2*pi*m*x+0.37))
       bases.append(np.cos(2*pi*m*x+1.13))
    B=np.array(bases, dtype=np.float64)
    starts=[]; deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit; count=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       sig=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.8))
       coeff=rng.normal(0.0, sig, B.shape[0])
       mask=rng.random(B.shape[0])<0.3
       coeff[mask]=0.0
       z=B.dot(coeff)
       z=np.clip(z,-6.0,6.0)
       a=np.exp(z); s=a.sum()
       if s<=0: a=np.ones(n)/n
       else: a=a/s
       sc=_score_norm(a)
       if np.isfinite(sc):
          starts.append((sc,count,a))
          count +=1
          if len(starts)>80:
             starts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
             del starts[80:]
    if not starts: return [], np.inf
    starts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    return [a for _,_,a in starts[:12]], starts[0][0]
```
But no lambda? Rule says no lambda functions. Need avoid lambda. Use `starts.sort()` since tuples sort by first then second. Good. We'll not use lambda. For del, if starts is list of tuples sorted lexicographic fine.

`_adam_optimize` implementation details. Let's write carefully:
```
def _adam_optimize(a0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n < 2 or budget_s <= 0:
       c=_autocorr(a); return a, _score_norm(a,c)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    loga -= loga.max()
    c=_autocorr(a); mc=float(c.max()); score=float(2*n*mc)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    mstat=np.zeros(n); vstat=np.zeros(n); t=1
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999; lr=0.7; temp=0.45
    min_temp=0.015; max_temp=1.2; min_lr=0.05; max_lr=2.0
    no_improve=0; it=0
    polish_interval=8.0
    last_polish=time.monotonic()
    deadline=time.monotonic()+float(budget_s)
    print(f"[{tag}] start score={best_score:.6f} n={n}")
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # polish every interval if enough time
       if time.monotonic()-last_polish > polish_interval and deadline-time.monotonic() > 60:
          ap, scp, cp = _coordinate_polish(a, c, 2.5, rng, topK=160)
          last_polish=time.monotonic()
          if scp < best_score:
             best_a=ap.copy(); best_score=scp
          if scp < score - 1e-14:
             a=_normalize(ap); c=cp; mc=float(c.max()); score=float(2*n*mc)
             no_improve=0
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       # active weights
       if no_improve <= 8:
           p=_active_weights(c, temp, 0)
       else:
           # blend: topK with small temp
           K= int(min(c.size, max(32, 50)))
           p=_active_weights(c, max(0.05,temp*0.5), K)
       sgrad=_weighted_gradient(p,a)
       mean=float(np.dot(a,sgrad))
       h=sgrad - mean
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
       if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-14: scale=1e-14
       h=h/scale
       mstat=b1*mstat+(1-b1)*h
       vstat=b2*vstat+(1-b1)*h*h
       bc1=1-b1**t; bc2=1-b2**t
       step=lr*(mstat/bc1)/np.sqrt(vstat/bc2+1e-8)
       np.clip(step, -1.5, 1.5, out=step)
       loga = loga - step
       loga -= loga.max()
       np.clip(loga, -100.0, 100.0, out=loga)
       a=np.exp(loga); ssum=float(a.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 1e-300:
           a=_normalize(best_a)
           loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); loga-=loga.max()
       else: a/=ssum
       c=_autocorr(a); mc=float(c.max()); score=float(2*n*mc)
       if score < best_score:
           best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); improved=True
           # adapt
           if no_improve <=0: ...
           no_improve=0
           temp=max(min_temp, temp*0.98)
           lr=min(max_lr, lr*1.002)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve % 10 ==0:
              temp=min(max_temp, temp*1.15)
              lr=min(max_lr, lr*1.08)
           # KKT step
           if no_improve > 50 and (it % 15 == 0):
              # try KKT target
              p2=_active_weights(c, 0.2, 64)
              q=0.5*_weighted_gradient(p2,a)
              target=-np.log(q+1e-12)
              mt=float(np.max(target))
              if np.isfinite(mt):
                 target=target-mt; np.clip(target,-100,100,out=target)
                 for alpha in (0.1,0.3,0.6):
                    zt=(1-alpha)*loga + alpha*target
                    zt-=zt.max(); np.clip(zt,-100,100,out=zt)
                    aa=np.exp(zt); sam=float(aa.sum())
                    if not finite... continue
                    aa/=sam
                    cc=_autocorr(aa); mc2=float(cc.max()); sc2=float(2*n*mc2)
                    if sc2 < score:
                       a=aa; loga=zt; c=cc; mc=mc2; score=sc2; improved=True; break
              if improved:
                 # reset stats partially
                 mstat.fill(0); vstat.fill(0); t=1; no_improve=0; temp=0.3; lr=0.6
           if no_improve > 150:
              # perturb best
              a=_perturb(best_a, rng)
              loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); loga-=loga.max()
              c=_autocorr(a); mc=float(c.max()); score=float(2*n*mc)
              mstat.fill(0); vstat.fill(0); t=1; no_improve=0; temp=0.35; lr=0.6
       it +=1; t +=1
       if it % 1000 ==0:
          print(f"[{tag}] it={it} best={best_score:.6f} cur={score:.6f} temp={temp:.3f}")
    c=_autocorr(a); mc=float(c.max()); score=float(2*n*mc)
    if score < best_score: best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    print(f"[{tag}] end best={best_score:.6f}")
    return _normalize(best_a), best_score
```

def _perturb(a0, rng):
   a0=_normalize(a0); n=a0.size
   r=float(rng.random())
   if r<0.5:
      sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.8))
      z=np.log(np.maximum(a0,1e-300))
      z += sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)
      z -= z.max(); a=np.exp(np.clip(z,-100,100)); s=a.sum(); return a/s
   elif r<0.85:
      alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.5))
      noise=rng.random(n); snoise=noise.sum(); noise=noise/snoise if snoise>0 else ones/n
      a=(1-alpha)*a0 + alpha*noise; return _normalize(a)
   else:
      # shift/warp
      return _interp_to_length(a0, n) # no. Use smooth? 
      sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5)); z=log(a0)+sigma*normal; return normalize
```

In `_coordinate_polish`, we need efficient and not too complex. Let's adapt previous but with `topK` param. Avoid lambda. Set constants.
```
def _coordinate_polish(f0, c0, budget_s, rng, topK=128):
    f=_normalize(f0); n=f.size
    if n<2 or budget_s<=0: c=_autocorr(f); return f, _score_norm(f,c), c
    L=2*n-1
    try: c=np.asarray(c0,dtype=float).copy()
    except: c=_autocorr(f)
    if c.size != L or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): c=_autocorr(f)
    maxc=float(c.max()); best_f=f.copy(); best_score=float(2*n*maxc)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    delta=np.zeros(L); total=np.zeros(L)
    no_improve=0; moves=0
    # constants
    if n>5000: D=R=5; topK=min(L,max(64, topK))
    elif n>1000: D=R=7; ...
    else: D=R=10
    fracs=(0.02,0.05,0.12,0.3,0.7)
    random_trials=12 if n>5000 else 20
    while time < deadline and no_improve < 12:
       maxc=float(c.max())
       if maxc <=0: break
       p=_active_weights(c, 0.2, min(L, topK))
       g=_weighted_gradient(p,f)
       # donors top D nonzero
       donor_order=np.argsort(g)[::-1]
       donors=[]
       for idx in donor_order:
          idx=int(idx)
          if f[idx] > 1e-12:
             donors.append(idx); if len==D: break
       if not donors: break
       donor_set=set(donors)
       recv_order=np.argsort(g)
       receivers=[]
       for idx in recv_order:
          idx=int(idx); if idx not in donor_set:
             receivers.append(idx); if len==R: break
       if not receivers: no_improve++; continue
       # precompute unit impacts? We can compute for each donor/receiver as arrays?
       # To save memory, for each pair compute delta by setting slices? Instead of ui/uj arrays, we can fill delta zeros and set f slices each pair? That is slower.
       # Old method: precompute unit arrays for donors and receivers: u length L with -f or +f at shifted region. But that is D+R arrays length L, ~10*16k small.
       donor_units=[]
       for i in donors:
          u=np.zeros(L); u[i:i+n] = -f; donor_units.append((i,u))
       recv_units=[]
       for j in receivers:
          u=np.zeros(L); u[j:j+n] = f; recv_units.append((j,u))
       best_mc=maxc; best_move=None
       for i,ui in donor_units:
          fi=float(f[i])
          if fi <= 1e-12: continue
          for j,uj in recv_units:
             if i == j: continue
             for frac in fracs:
                dv=fi*frac
                if dv <=0: continue
                np.add(ui,uj,out=delta)
                delta *= (2.0*dv)
                q=dv*dv
                delta[2*j] += q
                delta[2*i] += q
                delta[i+j] -= 2.0*q
                np.add(c,delta,out=total)
                mc=float(np.max(total))
                if mc < best_mc:
                   best_mc=mc; best_move=(i,j,dv,delta.copy())
       # random fallback if no improvement or still near max
       if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc - 1e-15:
          for _ in range(random_trials):
             i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
             if i==j or f[i]<=1e-12: continue
             frac=float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.5))
             dv=f[i]*frac
             # direct fill delta
             delta.fill(0)
             delta[i:i+n] += -f
             delta[j:j+n] += f
             delta *= 2*dv
             q=dv*dv; delta[2*j]+=q; delta[2*i]+=q; delta[i+j]-=2*q
             np.add(c,delta,out=total); mc=float(total.max())
             if mc < best_mc: best_mc=mc; best_move=(i,j,dv,delta.copy())
       if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc - 1e-15:
          no_improve +=1; continue
       i,j,dv,d_saved=best_move
       f[i] -= dv
       if f[i] < 0: f[i]=0
       f[j] += dv
       c = c + d_saved
       np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
       # optional normalize? The transfer preserves sum exactly; due float. If moves%20==0: f=_normalize(f); c=_autocorr(f); maxc=...
       moves+=1; no_improve=0
       cur_score=float(2*n*best_mc)
       if cur_score < best_score: best_score=cur_score; best_f=f.copy()
       if moves % 25 ==0:
          f=_normalize(f); c=_autocorr(f); maxc=float(c.max())
          cur_score=float(2*n*maxc)
          if cur_score < best_score: best_score=cur_score; best_f=f.copy()
    f=_normalize(best_f); c=_autocorr(f); mc=float(c.max()); sc=float(2*n*mc)
    if sc > best_score: # due recompute? keep best
       bf=_normalize(best_f); bc=_autocorr(bf); bmc=...
    return ...
```
Need in random fallback, `delta[i:i+n] += -f` works? If i+n > L? i≤n-1, i+n≤2n-1=L, valid. For j. Good. But `delta[i:i+n] += -f` when i slice length n. Good. Then `delta *= 2*dv`. Quadratic as before. If overlapping slices, adding both gives correct linear delta. If i and j ranges overlap, sum correct. Good.

Old method with precomputed units: `np.add(ui,uj,out=delta)` leaves previous values? It sets out to ui+uj exactly, so fine. `delta *= 2dv`. Then quadratic updates in-place. Good.

In coordinate, if best_move delta.copy large; apply `c = c + d_saved` creates new. For many moves memory okay. Could `c += d_saved` in-place, but if d_saved later? We only need keep. Use `c = c + d_saved` to avoid mutating d_saved? no matter.

If `c` has negative tiny, clipping after move. But if we clip, sum c no longer exact; drift small. Recompute every 25 moves.

Potential cost: Precomputing D+R unit arrays each iteration allocates 10*16k ~160k floats 1.3MB, okay. But if many iterations, realloc. Could precompute f once? f changes each move; units depend on f. Need update. Fine.

Could use `np.zeros_like(c)` for units? each.

def _postprocess(a):
   variants=[]

   # reverse? same score, skip.

Function `_smooth_window(f, win)` not in previous? Define.

```
def _smooth_window(f, win):
   f=_normalize(f); n=f.size; win=int(win)
   if n<win or win<3: return f
   k=np.ones(win)/win
   b=np.convolve(f,k,mode='same')
   return _normalize(b)
```

In postprocess, if a length large, convolve smoothing O(n*win) small.

def _add_candidate(record_list, a, tag):
   a=_normalize(a)
   if a.size==0: return
   sc=_score_norm(a)
   if np.isfinite(sc): record_list.append([float(sc), a, tag])

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
  global height_sequence_1? No, just read.
  start=time.monotonic()
  deadline=start + max(60.0, float(budget_s)) - 90.0
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  # load f0
  try: f0=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
  except Exception: f0=np.ones(6121)
  f0=np.nan_to_num(f0, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
  np.clip(f0,0,1000,out=f0)
  f0=_normalize(f0)
  if f0.size<3: f0=np.ones(4096); 
  # final n
  if budget_s > 1500: N=8192
  elif budget_s > 800: N=4096
  else: N=2048
  records=[]
  _add_candidate(records, f0, "given")
  _add_candidate(records, _interp_to_length(f0,N), "givenN")
  _add_candidate(records, np.ones(N), "uniformN")
  _add_candidate(records, np.ones(f0.size), "uniform0")
  # quick random scan
  if deadline - time.monotonic() > 60:
     scdess = _quick_random_scan(N, rng, min(60.0, 0.03*budget_s))
     for i,a in enumerate(scdess): _add_candidate(records,a,f"rand{i}")
  # coarse if time
  if deadline - time.monotonic() > 800:
     nC=1024
     coarse_rec=[]
     _add_candidate(coarse_rec, _interp_to_length(f0,nC), "coarse given")
     # quick coarse random scan 10s
     ca_list, _ = _quick_random_scan(nC, rng, 10)
     for a in ca_list[:3]: _add_candidate(coarse_rec,a,"coarse rand")
     coarse_rec.sort()
     best_c = coarse_rec[0][1] if coarse_rec else _interp_to_length(f0,nC)
     best_sc = ...
     # optimize up to 3 starts
     for idx in range(min(3,len(coarse_rec))):
        rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if rem < 120: break
        alloc=min(45.0, rem*0.2)
        aC, scC = _adam_optimize(coarse_rec[idx][1], alloc, rng, f"coarse{idx}")
        if scC < best_sc: best_sc=scC; best_c=aC
     # lift to 2048 and optimize
     a2048=_interp_to_length(best_c, 2048)
     rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
     if rem>150:
        a2048,sc2048 = _adam_optimize(a2048, min(90.0, (rem-60)*0.35), rng, "mid2048")
        aN,_interp_to_length(a2048,N)
        rem=...
        if rem>120: aN,scN = _adam_optimize(aN, min(90.0,(rem-60)*0.35), rng,"lift")
        _add_candidate(records,aN,"coarse lift")
  # sort records
  records.sort()
  if not records: fallback uniform
  # main Adam: choose top distinct candidates by score and tag length
  # We'll run first candidate, then second if score close and different tag/length, then best.
  # To avoid duplicate exact arrays, only tag maybe.
  main_candidates=[]
  seen_tags=set()
  for rec in records:
     if rec[2] not in seen_tags: main_candidates.append(rec); seen_tags.add(rec[2])
     if len(main_candidates)>=3: break
  # allocate times
  total_main = min(1600, max(0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 650)) # reserve coord
  if total_main > 100:
     # weights
     if len(main_candidates)>=3: weights=[0.55,0.30,0.15]
     elif len==2: [0.65,0.35]
     else: [1.0]
     for idx,(sc,a,tag) in enumerate(main_candidates):
         rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
         if rem < 120: break
         frac = weights[idx]/sum(weights[idx:])
         alloc = max(60, min(rem-120, 0.85*total_main*frac))
         # if first candidate not getting all
         a_opt, sc_opt = _adam_optimize(a, alloc, rng, f"main{idx}_{tag}")
         _add_candidate(records, a_opt, f"main{idx}_{tag}")
  # coordinate polish current best
  records.sort(); best_a=records[0][1]; best_score=records[0][0]
  # If best_score > TARGET maybe coordinate longer
  rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
  coord_time = min(450.0, max(0.0, rem*0.45))
  if coord_time > 30:
     a_pol, sc_pol, c_pol = _coordinate_polish(best_a, _autocorr(best_a), coord_time, rng, topK=256)
     _add_candidate(records, a_pol, "coord1")
  # extra Adam from best
  records.sort(); best_a=records[0][1]
  rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
  extra_grad = min(250.0, max(0.0, rem*0.35))
  if extra_grad > 60:
     a_ex, sc_ex = _adam_optimize(best_a, extra_grad, rng, "extra")
     _add_candidate(records, a_ex, "extra")
  # final coordinate
  records.sort(); best_a=records[0][1]
  rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
  coord_time2 = min(400.0, max(0.0, rem*0.5))
  if coord_time2 > 30:
     a_pol2, sc_pol2, _ = _coordinate_polish(best_a, _autocorr(best_a), coord_time2, rng, topK=256)
     _add_candidate(records, a_pol2, "coord2")
  # postprocess
  records.sort(); best_a=records[0][1]
  final_a, final_sc = _postprocess(best_a)
  _add_candidate(records, final_a, "post")
  records.sort()
  # Choose best finite; if official maybe
  best_a=records[0][1]; best_score=records[0][0]
  out=[float(x) for x in best_a]
  # ensure finite? evaluate internal
  official=np.inf
  try:
      official=float(evaluate_sequence(out))
  except Exception: official=np.inf
  if np.isfinite(official) and official < best_score + 1e-12:
      best_score=official
  print(f"FINAL internal={best_score:.6f} official={official:.6f} n={len(out)}")
  return out
```

def _postprocess(a):
    a=_normalize(a)
    c=_autocorr(a); best_sc=_score_norm(a,c); best_a=a.copy()
    # zero pruning
    maxa=float(a.max())
    for frac in (1e-5,1e-4,5e-4,2e-3):
        b=a.copy()
        thr = float(np.quantile(b[ b>0 ], frac)) if np.any(b>0) else 0
        b[b < thr] = 0.0
        b=_normalize(b)
        sc=_score_norm(b)
        if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=b.copy()
    # smoothing
    for win in (3,5,11):
        if a.size >= win*2:
           b=_smooth_window(a,win); sc=_score_norm(b); update
    # combine smoothing with pruning? no
    return best_a, float(best_sc)
```

        return p/s
```
If violation=0.999 and only max equal thr? If max*0.999 < max, p positive for max; if multiple max. Good. If all c below thr? thr<max if max>0 and violation<1. Good.

`_active_weights` softmax branch: `denom = mc * max(1e-12, float(temp))`. If temp=0? max. z=(c-mc)/denom, max 0, min maybe large negative; clip -80. Good.

`_coordinate_polish` constants: for n>1000 D=R=6 maybe. Use if n>8000 D=5. Let's set:
```
if n > 8000: D=R=5
elif n > 2000: D=R=6
else: D=R=8
```
fracs length 5. Random trials 10/18. Good.

In `_coordinate_polish`, after applying best_move, if `moves % 25 ==0` recompute. But after recompute, if `f=_normalize(f)` changes values, the `d_saved` not. Fine.

Potential bug: `c = c + d_saved` uses `c` old, but if we clipped c before, d_saved computed from clipped c? We compute delta using current c (possibly clipped) capped; exact linear/quadratic from true f. If c had tiny negative clipped positive? Actually clipping sets negative to 0, so c >= exact; delta from f true may produce total negative? We clip after. Drift fixed by recompute.

Potential bug in coordinate: If `best_move` is found from a random fallback using delta filled with slices from current f, and we store copy. Good.

Now, in `_postprocess`, after zero pruning, if thr=0 for frac small, b same. It computes score. Good.

Now, final exact score selection: records may include many with same a? Not issue. If exact score is slower and time close, we guard. Code:
```
if time.monotonic() < deadline and best_a.size <= 15000:
   for rec in records[:min(6,len(records))]:
      if deadline - time.monotonic() < 8: break
      a=rec[1]
      try: sc=_exact_score(a)
      except: continue
      if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score: ...
```
Good.

One more potential improvement: Use exact score in `_postprocess` if n≤20000 and time? Not needed.

Let's now produce final code. Ensure final only code block.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function sequence minimizing
    2*n*max_k conv(a,a)_k / (sum a)^2.

The program uses:
  - fast FFT-based convolution scoring,
  - low-dimensional random shape scanning for diverse starts,
  - an annealed mirror-descent / Adam optimizer in log-domain,
  - KKT-style targeted corrections when stagnating,
  - exact two-coordinate transfer polish,
  - coarse-to-fine lifting,
  - final exact-convolution verification and pruning/smoothing post-process.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027


def _normalize(v):
    """Project to a non-negative float vector with sum 1."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    v[~np.isfinite(v)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(v, 0.0, out=v)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(v.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(v.size)
    v /= s
    return v


def _fft_size(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m, at least 2."""
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 2:
        return 2
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _autocorr(f):
    """Full linear convolution f*f via FFT, clipped to non-negative."""
    n = f.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = _fft_size(m)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, size)
    F *= F
    full = np.fft.irfft(F, size)
    full = np.maximum(full[:m], 0.0)
    return full


def _exact_score(a):
    """Exact score using direct convolution. Use only when n is moderate."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(np.max(c)))


def _score_norm(a, c=None):
    """Score for a normalized non-negative vector."""
    if c is None:
        c = _autocorr(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()))


def _fast_score(a):
    """Normalize and score."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    return _score_norm(a)


def _weighted_gradient(p, f):
    """
    Return 2 * conv(p, f_reversed) sampled at indices [n-1, n+n-2],
    i.e. g_i = 2 * sum_k p_k f_{k-i} (valid indices only).
    """
    n = f.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = p.size
    m = L + n - 1
    size = _fft_size(m)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], size)
    P *= F
    conv = np.fft.irfft(P, size)
    return 2.0 * conv[n - 1:n + n - 1]


def _active_weights(c, temp, topK=0, violation=0.0):
    """
    Builds a non-negative weight vector p over convolution coefficients.
    Modes:
      violation > 0: p_k = (c_k - violation*max(c))_+
      topK > 0:      support restricted to the largest topK coefficients
      otherwise:     softmax with temperature temp * max(c)
    """
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if violation > 0.0:
        mc = float(c.max())
        if mc > 0.0:
            thr = mc * float(violation)
            p = np.maximum(c - thr, 0.0)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-300:
                return p / s

    if topK is None or topK <= 0 or topK >= L:
        mc = float(c.max())
        if mc <= 0.0:
            p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
            return p
        denom = mc * max(1e-12, float(temp))
        z = (c - mc) / denom
        np.clip(z, -80.0, 0.0, out=z)
        w = np.exp(z)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            return p
        return w / s

    K = int(min(L, max(1, int(topK))))
    if K >= L:
        s = float(c.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            return c / s
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)

    idx = L - K
    thr = float(np.partition(c, idx)[idx])
    p = c.copy()
    p[p < thr] = 0.0
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _interp_to_length(f, n):
    """Linearly resample a non-negative sequence to length n and normalize."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    m = f.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return f
    if m == 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, m, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1, n, dtype=np.float64)
    return _normalize(np.interp(new, old, f))


def _smooth_window(f, win):
    """Uniform moving-average smoothing, normalized."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = f.size
    win = int(win)
    if n < 3 or win < 3:
        return f
    if win > n:
        win = max(3, n // 2)
    k = np.ones(win, dtype=np.float64) / float(win)
    return _normalize(np.convolve(f, k, mode="same"))


def _random_shape(n, rng):
    """A random smooth non-negative shape in log-amplitude domain."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = x - 0.5
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    deg = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
    sig = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 2.0))
    for d in range(1, deg + 1):
        z += float(rng.normal(0.0, sig)) * (u ** d)

    if rng.random() < 0.75:
        z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.2)) * np.log(x + 1e-3)
        z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.2)) * np.log(1.0 - x + 1e-3)

    if rng.random() < 0.75:
        m = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.8)) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            phase2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6)) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase2)

    z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.3))
    z = np.clip(z, -6.0, 6.0)
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _quick_random_scan(n, rng, time_limit):
    """
    Fast random search over a small polynomial + log + sinusoidal basis.
    Returns a list of good candidate arrays (best first) and best score.
    """
    if n <= 0 or time_limit <= 0:
        return [], np.inf

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = x - 0.5
    bases = [
        np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
        u,
        u * u,
        u ** 3,
        u ** 4,
        np.log(x + 1e-3),
        np.log(1.0 - x + 1e-3),
    ]
    for m in (1, 2, 3):
        bases.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + 0.37))
        bases.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + 1.13))
    B = np.asarray(bases, dtype=np.float64)

    starts = []
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        sig = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.8))
        coeff = rng.normal(0.0, sig, B.shape[0])
        mask = rng.random(B.shape[0]) < 0.30
        coeff[mask] = 0.0

        z = B.dot(coeff)
        z = np.clip(z, -6.0, 6.0)
        a = np.exp(z)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        else:
            a = a / s

        sc = _score_norm(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            starts.append((float(sc), count, a))
            count += 1
            if len(starts) > 80:
                starts.sort()
                del starts[80:]

    if not starts:
        return [], np.inf
    starts.sort()
    return [a for _, _, a in starts[:12]], float(starts[0][0])


def _perturb(a0, rng):
    """Perturb a sequence in log/random/mixed ways, normalized."""
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    n = a0.size
    r = float(rng.random())

    if r < 0.55:
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.90))
        z = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
        z += sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
        z -= float(np.max(z))
        z = np.clip(z, -100.0, 100.0)
        a = np.exp(z)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return a0
        return a / s

    if r < 0.85:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.50))
        noise = rng.random(n)
        s = float(noise.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            noise /= s
        else:
            noise = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return _normalize((1.0 - alpha) * a0 + alpha * noise)

    sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.55))
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
    z += sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -100.0, 100.0)
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return a0
    return a / s


def _coordinate_polish(f0, c0, budget_s, rng, topK=128):
    """
    Local exact search by non-negative two-coordinate mass transfers.
    Returns (best_f, best_score, best_c).
    """
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n < 2 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        c = _autocorr(f)
        return f, _score_norm(f, c), c

    L = 2 * n - 1
    try:
        c = np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        c = _autocorr(f)
    if c.size != L or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c = _autocorr(f)

    maxc = float(c.max())
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget_s)
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    total = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    no_improve = 0
    moves = 0

    if n > 8000:
        D = 5
        R = 5
        topK = int(min(L, max(64, int(topK))))
    elif n > 2000:
        D = 6
        R = 6
        topK = int(min(L, max(96, int(topK))))
    else:
        D = 8
        R = 8
        topK = int(min(L, max(128, int(topK))))

    fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.12, 0.30, 0.70)
    random_trials = 10 if n > 5000 else 18

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 12:
        maxc = float(c.max())
        if maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        p = _active_weights(c, 0.20, topK, violation=0.985)
        g = _weighted_gradient(p, f)

        donor_order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
        donors = []
        for idx in donor_order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if f[idx] > 1e-12:
                donors.append(idx)
                if len(donors) >= D:
                    break

        if not donors:
            break

        donor_set = set(donors)
        recv_order = np.argsort(g)
        receivers = []
        for idx in recv_order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if idx not in donor_set:
                receivers.append(idx)
                if len(receivers) >= R:
                    break

        if not receivers:
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        donor_units = []
        for i in donors:
            u = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            u[i:i + n] = -f
            donor_units.append((i, u))

        recv_units = []
        for j in receivers:
            u = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            u[j:j + n] = f
            recv_units.append((j, u))

        best_mc = maxc
        best_move = None

        for i, ui in donor_units:
            fi = float(f[i])
            if fi <= 1e-12:
                continue
            for j, uj in recv_units:
                if i == j:
                    continue
                for frac in fracs:
                    dv = fi * float(frac)
                    if dv <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    np.add(ui, uj, out=delta)
                    delta *= (2.0 * dv)
                    q = dv * dv
                    delta[2 * j] += q
                    delta[2 * i] += q
                    delta[i + j] -= 2.0 * q
                    np.add(c, delta, out=total)
                    mc = float(np.max(total))
                    if mc < best_mc:
                        best_mc = mc
                        best_move = (i, j, dv, delta.copy())

        if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc - 1e-15:
            for _ in range(random_trials):
                i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                if i == j or f[i] <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                frac = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.55))
                dv = float(f[i]) * frac
                delta.fill(0.0)
                delta[i:i + n] += -f
                delta[j:j + n] += f
                delta *= (2.0 * dv)
                q = dv * dv
                delta[2 * j] += q
                delta[2 * i] += q
                delta[i + j] -= 2.0 * q
                np.add(c, delta, out=total)
                mc = float(np.max(total))
                if mc < best_mc:
                    best_mc = mc
                    best_move = (i, j, dv, delta.copy())

        if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc - 1e-15:
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        i, j, dv, d_saved = best_move
        f[i] -= dv
        if f[i] < 0.0:
            f[i] = 0.0
        f[j] += dv
        c = c + d_saved
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)

        moves += 1
        no_improve = 0
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * best_mc)
        if cur_score < best_score:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_f = f.copy()

        if moves % 25 == 0:
            f = _normalize(f)
            c = _autocorr(f)
            maxc = float(c.max())
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_f = f.copy()

    f = _normalize(best_f)
    c = _autocorr(f)
    sc = _score_norm(f, c)
    return f, float(sc), c


def _adam_optimize(a0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    """
    Long-horizon mirror-descent/Adam optimizer in log-domain.
    Includes KKT-style corrections, annealed active sets, and periodic
    coordinate polish.
    """
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        c = _autocorr(a)
        return a, _score_norm(a, c)

    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    loga -= float(np.max(loga))

    c = _autocorr(a)
    mc = float(c.max())
    score = float(2.0 * n * mc)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    mstat = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    vstat = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 1
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999

    lr = 0.70
    temp = 0.45
    min_lr = 0.05
    max_lr = 2.00
    min_temp = 0.015
    max_temp = 1.20

    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_polish = time.monotonic()
    polish_interval = 8.0

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget_s)
    print(f"[{tag}] start score={best_score:.6f} n={n}", flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if (time.monotonic() - last_polish > polish_interval
                and deadline - time.monotonic() > 60.0):
            ap, scp, cp = _coordinate_polish(a, c, 2.5, rng, topK=160)
            last_polish = time.monotonic()
            if scp < best_score:
                best_a = ap.copy()
                best_score = scp
            if scp < score - 1e-14:
                a = _normalize(ap)
                c = cp
                mc = float(c.max())
                score = float(2.0 * n * mc)
                no_improve = 0

        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        score_before = score

        if no_improve <= 8:
            p = _active_weights(c, temp, 0, 0.0)
        else:
            vio = 0.96 + 0.04 * min(1.0, no_improve / 100.0)
            vio = min(0.999, vio)
            p = _active_weights(c, max(0.05, temp * 0.5), 0, vio)

        sgrad = _weighted_gradient(p, a)
        mean = float(np.dot(a, sgrad))
        h = sgrad - mean
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-14:
            scale = 1e-14
        h = h / scale

        mstat = b1 * mstat + (1.0 - b1) * h
        vstat = b2 * vstat + (1.0 - b2) * (h * h)
        bc1 = 1.0 - b1 ** t
        bc2 = 1.0 - b2 ** t
        step = lr * (mstat / bc1) / np.sqrt(vstat / bc2 + 1e-8)
        np.clip(step, -1.5, 1.5, out=step)

        loga = loga - step
        loga -= float(np.max(loga))
        np.clip(loga, -100.0, 100.0, out=loga)

        a = np.exp(loga)
        ss = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 1e-300:
            a = _normalize(best_a)
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            loga -= float(np.max(loga))
        else:
            a = a / ss

        c = _autocorr(a)
        mc = float(c.max())
        score = float(2.0 * n * mc)

        if not np.isfinite(score):
            a = _normalize(best_a)
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            loga -= float(np.max(loga))
            c = _autocorr(a)
            score = _score_norm(a, c)
            no_improve = 200
            it += 1
            t += 1
            continue

        improved_local = False
        if score < score_before - 1e-14:
            improved_local = True

        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()

        # KKT-style correction when stuck: log a_i ~ -log(q_i)
        if no_improve > 50 and (it % 15 == 0):
            p2 = _active_weights(c, 0.20, 64, violation=0.98)
            q = 0.5 * _weighted_gradient(p2, a)
            target = -np.log(q + 1e-12)
            mt = float(np.max(target))
            if np.isfinite(mt):
                target = target - mt
                np.clip(target, -100.0, 100.0, out=target)
                for alpha in (0.10, 0.30, 0.60):
                    zt = (1.0 - alpha) * loga + alpha * target
                    zt -= float(np.max(zt))
                    np.clip(zt, -100.0, 100.0, out=zt)
                    aa = np.exp(zt)
                    sam = float(aa.sum())
                    if not np.isfinite(sam) or sam <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    aa = aa / sam
                    cc = _autocorr(aa)
                    mc2 = float(cc.max())
                    sc2 = float(2.0 * n * mc2)
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < score - 1e-14:
                        a = aa
                        loga = zt
                        c = cc
                        mc = mc2
                        score = sc2
                        improved_local = True
                        if score < best_score:
                            best_score = score
                            best_a = a.copy()
                        break

        if improved_local:
            no_improve = 0
            temp = max(min_temp, temp * 0.985)
            lr = min(max_lr, lr * 1.002)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 10 == 0:
                temp = min(max_temp, temp * 1.15)
                lr = min(max_lr, lr * 1.08)

        if no_improve > 160:
            a = _perturb(best_a, rng)
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            loga -= float(np.max(loga))
            c = _autocorr(a)
            mc = float(c.max())
            score = float(2.0 * n * mc)
            mstat.fill(0.0)
            vstat.fill(0.0)
            t = 1
            no_improve = 0
            temp = 0.35
            lr = 0.60

        it += 1
        t += 1

        if it % 1000 == 0:
            print(f"[{tag}] it={it} best={best_score:.6f} cur={score:.6f} temp={temp:.3f}", flush=True)

    c = _autocorr(a)
    score = _score_norm(a, c)
    if score < best_score:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_score = score

    print(f"[{tag}] end best={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)
    return _normalize(best_a), float(best_score)


def _postprocess(a):
    """Small safe post-processing: zero-pruning and light smoothing."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    c = _autocorr(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_norm(a, c)

    # Try pruning the tiniest entries.
    pos = a[a > 0]
    if pos.size > 0:
        for frac in (1e-5, 1e-4, 5e-4, 2e-3):
            b = a.copy()
            thr = float(np.quantile(pos, frac))
            if thr <= 0.0:
                continue
            b[b < thr] = 0.0
            b = _normalize(b)
            sc = _score_norm(b)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = b.copy()

    # Very light smoothing of possible local roughness.
    for win in (3, 5, 11):
        if a.size >= 2 * win:
            b = _smooth_window(a, win)
            sc = _score_norm(b)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = b.copy()

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def _add_candidate(records, a, tag):
    """Evaluate and add a candidate, if finite."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    sc = _score_norm(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        records.append([float(sc), a, str(tag)])


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search and return a Python list of non-negative floats.
    """
    try:
        start_t = time.monotonic()
        run_time = max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 80.0)
        deadline = start_t + run_time
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

        try:
            f0 = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            f0 = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)

        f0 = np.nan_to_num(f0, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        np.clip(f0, 0.0, 1000.0, out=f0)
        f0 = _normalize(f0)

        if f0.size < 3:
            f0 = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)

        if budget_s > 1500:
            N = 8192
        elif budget_s > 800:
            N = 4096
        else:
            N = 2048

        records = []
        _add_candidate(records, f0, "given")
        _add_candidate(records, _interp_to_length(f0, N), "given_N")
        _add_candidate(records, np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64), "uniform_N")
        _add_candidate(records, np.ones(f0.size, dtype=np.float64), "uniform_given_len")

        # Quick shape scan at target resolution.
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 60:
            scan_list, _ = _quick_random_scan(N, rng, min(60.0, 0.03 * float(budget_s)))
            for i, a in enumerate(scan_list):
                _add_candidate(records, a, f"scan{i}")

        # Coarse-to-fine search if time allows.
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 900:
            nC = 1024
            coarse = []
            _add_candidate(coarse, _interp_to_length(f0, nC), "coarse_given")
            coarse_scan, _ = _quick_random_scan(nC, rng, 12.0)
            for i, a in enumerate(coarse_scan[:4]):
                _add_candidate(coarse, a, f"coarse_rand{i}")
            coarse.sort()

            if coarse:
                best_c = coarse[0][1].copy()
                best_sc = best_c and coarse[0][0]
                idxs = range(min(3, len(coarse)))
                for idx in idxs:
                    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if rem < 120:
                        break
                    alloc = min(45.0, rem * 0.25)
                    aC, scC = _adam_optimize(coarse[idx][1], alloc, rng, f"coarse{idx}")
                    if scC < best_sc:
                        best_sc = scC
                        best_c = aC.copy()

                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 220:
                    aM = _interp_to_length(best_c, 2048)
                    aM, scM = _adam_optimize(aM, min(90.0, rem * 0.35), rng, "mid2048")
                    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if rem > 160:
                        aN = _interp_to_length(aM, N)
                        aN, scN = _adam_optimize(aN, min(90.0, rem * 0.35), rng, "lift")
                        _add_candidate(records, aN, "coarse_lift")

        # Main mirror-descent runs from the best few distinct starts.
        records.sort()
        main_candidates = []
        seen = set()
        for rec in records:
            tag = rec[2]
            if tag not in seen:
                main_candidates.append(rec)
                seen.add(tag)
                if len(main_candidates) >= 3:
                    break

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        total_main = min(1600.0, max(0.0, rem - 700.0))
        if total_main > 120 and main_candidates:
            if len(main_candidates) >= 3:
                weights = [0.55, 0.30, 0.15]
            elif len(main_candidates) == 2:
                weights = [0.65, 0.35]
            else:
                weights = [1.0]
            weights = weights[:len(main_candidates)]

            for idx, rec in enumerate(main_candidates):
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem < 120:
                    break
                if idx < len(weights):
                    rem_w = sum(weights[idx:])
                    frac = weights[idx] / rem_w if rem_w > 0 else 0.0
                else:
                    frac = 0.0
                alloc = max(60.0, min(rem - 90.0, 0.90 * total_main * frac))
                if alloc <= 0:
                    continue
                a_opt, sc_opt = _adam_optimize(rec[1], alloc, rng, f"main{idx}_{rec[2]}")
                _add_candidate(records, a_opt, f"main{idx}_{rec[2]}")

        # Dedicated coordinate polish.
        records.sort()
        if records:
            best_a = records[0][1].copy()
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            coord1 = min(500.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.45))
            if coord1 > 30:
                ap, scp, _ = _coordinate_polish(best_a, _autocorr(best_a), coord1, rng, topK=256)
                _add_candidate(records, ap, "coord1")

            records.sort()
            if records:
                best_a = records[0][1].copy()
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                extra_grad = min(220.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.30))
                if extra_grad > 60:
                    aE, scE = _adam_optimize(best_a, extra_grad, rng, "extra")
                    _add_candidate(records, aE, "extra")

                records.sort()
                if records:
                    best_a = records[0][1].copy()
                    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    coord2 = min(500.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.50))
                    if coord2 > 30:
                        ap2, sc2, _ = _coordinate_polish(best_a, _autocorr(best_a), coord2, rng, topK=256)
                        _add_candidate(records, ap2, "coord2")

        # Final safe post-processing.
        records.sort()
        if not records:
            final_a = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
            final_score = np.inf
        else:
            final_a, final_score = _postprocess(records[0][1].copy())
            _add_candidate(records, final_a, "post")
            records.sort()
            final_a = records[0][1].copy()
            final_score = records[0][0]

        # If enough time and length is moderate, verify the best few using
        # direct convolution to match the evaluator more exactly.
        if final_a.size <= 15000:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            for _rec in records[:min(6, len(records))]:
                if rem < 8.0:
                    break
                a_cand = _rec[1]
                try:
                    sc_exact = _exact_score(a_cand)
                except Exception:
                    sc_exact = np.inf
                if np.isfinite(sc_exact) and sc_exact < final_score:
                    final_score = float(sc_exact)
                    final_a = a_cand.copy()
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()

        final_a = _normalize(final_a)
        final_a = np.nan_to_num(final_a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        np.clip(final_a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=final_a)
        final_a = _normalize(final_a)

        out = [float(x) for x in final_a]

        official = np.inf
        try:
            official = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            official = np.inf

        if np.isfinite(official) and official < final_score:
            final_score = float(official)

        print(f"FINAL internal score = {final_score:.6f}", flush=True)
        print(f"FINAL official score = {official:.6f}", flush=True)
        print(f"FINAL n = {len(out)}", flush=True)
        return out

    except Exception as exc:
        print("exception in propose_candidate:", repr(exc), flush=True)
        try:
            fb = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
            fb = np.nan_to_num(fb, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            np.clip(fb, 0.0, 1000.0, out=fb)
        except Exception:
            fb = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)
        fb = _normalize(fb)
        return [float(x) for x in fb]
```
